# Graph Report - thingtime  (2026-09-09)

## Corpus Check
- 2262 files · ~8,262,397 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 41504 nodes · 104690 edges · 1808 communities (979 shown, 829 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 98% EXTRACTED · 2% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2523 edges (avg confidence: 0.67)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `4aa8b8df`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
